--- Result 1 (score: 0.927) ---
File: src/users/service_0.py
Lines: 0-45
```python

class AuthHandler_0:
    """Handles authentication for module 0.
    
    This module provides authentication services including:
    - Token validation and refresh
    - Session management  
    - Permission checking
    - Rate limiting
    
    Note: This is part of the larger auth system that spans
    multiple modules and services. See also: auth_service_1.py
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: dict, db: Database):
        self.config = config
        self.db = db
        self.cache = Cache(ttl=config.get('cache_ttl', 3600))
        self.rate_limiter = RateLimiter(max_requests=100)
        
    async def validate_token(self, token: str) -> bool:
        """Validate JWT token against stored secrets."""
        if not token:
            return False
        try:
            payload = jwt.decode(token, self.config['secret'])
            return payload.get('exp', 0) > time.time()
        except jwt.InvalidTokenError:
            return False
            
    async def refresh_session(self, session_id: str) -> Session:
        """Refresh an existing session, extending its TTL."""
        session = await self.db.get_session(session_id)
        if session and session.is_valid():
            session.extend(hours=24)
            await self.db.save_session(session)
        return session

```

class AuthHandler_5:
    """Handles authentication for module 5.
    
    This module provides authentication services including:
    - Token validation and refresh
    - Session management  
    - Permission checking
    - Rate limiting
    
    Note: This is part of the larger auth system that spans
    multiple modules and services. See also: auth_service_6.py
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: dict, db: Database):
        self.config = config
        self.db = db
        self.cache = Cache(ttl=config.get('cache_ttl', 3600))
        self.rate_limiter = RateLimiter(max_requests=100)
        
    async def validate_token(self, token: str) -> bool:
        """Validate JWT token against stored secrets."""
        if not token:
            return False
        try:
            payload = jwt.decode(token, self.config['secret'])
            return payload.get('exp', 0) > time.time()

        self.config = config
        self.db = db
        self.cache = Cache(ttl=config.get('cache_ttl', 3600))
        self.rate_limiter = RateLimiter(max_requests=100)
        
    async def validate_token(self, token: str) -> bool:
        """Validate JWT token against stored secrets."""
        if not token:
            return False
        try:
            payload = jwt.decode(token, self.config['secret'])
            return payload.get('exp', 0) > time.time()
        except jwt.InvalidTokenError:
            return False
            
    async def refresh_session(self, session_id: str) -> Session:
        """Refresh an existing session, extending its TTL."""
        session = await self.db.get_session(session_id)
        if session and session.is_valid():
            session.extend(hours=24)
            await self.db.save_session(session)
        return session

```python

class AuthHandler_45:
    """Handles authentication for module 45.
    
    This module provides authentication services including:
    - Token validation and refresh
    - Session management  
    - Permission checking
    - Rate limiting
    
    Note: This is part of the larger auth system that spans
    multiple modules and services. See also: auth_service_46.py
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: dict, db: Database):
        self.config = config
        self.db = db
        self.cache = Cache(ttl=config.get('cache_ttl', 3600))
        self.rate_limiter = RateLimiter(max_requests=100)
        
    async def validate_token(self, token: str) -> bool:
        """Validate JWT token against stored secrets."""
        if not token:
            return False
        try:
            payload = jwt.decode(token, self.config['secret'])
            return payload.get('exp', 0) > time.time()
        except jwt.InvalidTokenError:
            return False
            
    async def refresh_session(self, session_id: str) -> Session:
        """Refresh an existing session, extending its TTL."""
        session = await self.db.get_session(session_id)
        if session and session.is_valid():
            session.extend(hours=24)
            await self.db.save_session(session)
        return session

```
